declare void @willrt_init()
declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t518 = call ptr @"example.com/concurrency/async.will$main.task"(i32 -2, i32 0)
  %t520 = alloca i32
  %t519 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t518, ptr %t520, ptr null)
  %t521 = icmp ne i32 %t519, 0
  br i1 %t521, label %main.async.fail.58, label %main.async.ok.58
main.async.fail.58:
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.58:
  %t522 = load i32, ptr %t520
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t522
}
@will.error.data.8 = private constant { ptr, i64, i32 } { ptr @.str.15, i64 8, i32 8 }
